Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [138] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，用于解决张拉整体结构找形和物理属性预测问题，通过将总势能最小化和本构关系融入训练目标，实现了对平衡节点构型和物理量的同时预测，提高了物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体结构找形和物理属性预测是结构力学中的基础反问题，由于几何与力的耦合导致的强非线性、结构稳定性保证以及边界条件和对称性等约束的执行，这些问题具有挑战性。此外，传统方法往往缺乏对噪声和异常值的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，将总势能最小化和本构关系纳入训练目标中，通过在学习过程中直接引入基于能量的物理损失，实现对平衡节点构型和相关物理量（包括构件力和力密度）的同时预测。

Result: 在张拉整体结构（包括棱柱和着陆器系统）上的数值实验表明，该方法在可扩展的找形和结构属性准确预测方面具有巨大潜力，展现了良好的物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于能量的学习框架成功解决了张拉整体结构找形和物理属性预测中的关键挑战，为结构力学反问题提供了一种有效的新方法，具有重要的应用前景和推广价值。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [139] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoCo损失函数，用于学习归一化和结构良好的表示，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时实现了更紧密的类内聚类和更快的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的损失函数（如点回归和交叉熵）在学习结构化表示时存在局限性，难以同时实现类内坍缩、类间对比和保持神经网络的灵活性。需要一种能够鼓励类内坍缩和类间对比，同时保持足够灵活性以逼近具有大类间角度分离的几何最优嵌入的损失函数。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，鼓励类内坍缩和类间对比，同时保持神经网络的灵活性以逼近几何最优嵌入。从理论上分析了CoCo与点回归、交叉熵等目标函数的关系，证明了新损失函数在初始化接近最优配置、梯度信息更丰富和更强的类表示坍缩激励方面的优势。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多样化表格数据集上，CoCo与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和基于交叉熵的神经网络等最先进方法取得了竞争性性能。理论论证和实证分析表明，该方法促进了更紧密的类聚类和更快的收敛。

Conclusion: CoCo损失是学习判别性表示的有效目标函数，同时保持了竞争性的预测性能。该损失函数通过更紧密的类聚类和更快的收敛，在学习结构化表示方面表现出色。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [140] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的随机预测器，突破了传统\(T^{2/3}\)的校准误差障碍，实现了期望校准误差\(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\)的改进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线二元序列校准问题中，传统方法长期存在\(T^{2/3}\)的校准误差障碍。近期Dagan等人(2024)的突破性工作打破了这一障碍，本文在此基础上进一步研究如何构建高效的预测器来实现更优的校准误差保证。

Method: 提出了一种结合SPR-Calibration过程和外部Blackwell式修正层的高效随机预测器。SPR-Calibration过程控制相对于条件均值估计代理序列的校准，修正层控制使用代理近似真实结果时产生的额外误差。分析将总校准误差分解为代理校准误差和代理序列与真实结果之间的残差偏差。

Result: 实现了期望校准误差\(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\)的保证，其中\(\varepsilon>0\)为常数，成功突破了传统的\(T^{2/3}\)障碍。代理校准误差通过SPR-Calibration保证进行界定，残差偏差通过二次势能论证和SPR-Calibration预测器的稀疏性进行控制。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙结合SPR-Calibration过程和Blackwell式修正层，成功构建了一个高效的随机预测器，在在线二元序列校准问题上实现了优于传统\(T^{2/3}\)障碍的校准误差性能，为该领域提供了新的理论进展。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [141] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于功率谱的不可预测性指标无法评估上下文扩展（如检索增强、基础模型）的价值，提出了覆盖率缺失诊断方法来区分二阶结构和高阶结构的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者常误将基于功率谱的不可预测性指标用于评估添加上下文（更长回溯窗口、检索插件、预训练模型）是否有帮助，但这是两个不同的问题。谱基指标在相位随机化下保持不变，而检索和基础模型提供的超二阶价值则会改变，因为相位随机化后的序列渐近于高斯分布。

Method: 提出不可可能性结果并通过代理对（surrogate pairs）进行隔离验证；引入无标签、配置层面的诊断方法——覆盖率缺失（coverage deficit），其主项通过模拟预测相对于线性预测的增益来测量超谱结构。在七个基准数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 检索的价值在代理对间崩溃（ECL中位数从+33%降至-35%，p<10^-40），而所有谱指标保持冻结；基础模型的价值分裂为存活的二阶部分和崩溃的小超线性边际；更长线性窗口的价值存活。结构项能预测超谱价值的符号，而谱指标在二阶机制上表现更好。

Conclusion: 本文未引入新的预测器，核心贡献是区分了两种预测机制、提供了受控比较方法和部署决策的诊断工具。谱基指标仅能评估线性方法价值，无法预测需要高阶统计量的扩展方法价值。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [142] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TerraZero，一个高效的过程化驾驶模拟器和自博弈训练框架，通过强化学习训练出在真实世界地图上具有强泛化能力的自动驾驶策略，在多个基准测试中取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练鲁棒的自动驾驶智能体需要满足三个关键条件的模拟器：足够快以支持大规模强化学习、足够真实以基于真实世界地图结构、足够多样化以覆盖日志数据中罕见的各种安全关键的“长尾”场景。现有的模拟器难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 开发了TerraZero过程化驾驶模拟器和自博弈训练栈。主要方法包括：1）可配置的C++引擎在CPU上运行模拟，在GPU上进行策略推理，通过零拷贝路径实现每秒130万步的高效模拟；2）仅将日志数据作为真实世界地图几何来源，用随机化的规则道路用户、信号控制器填充地图，并随机化智能体动力学、奖励和尺寸，生成无限场景集；3）采用纯强化学习自博弈方法训练，无需人类示范或推论时的后备规划器。

Result: 策略实现零样本跨城市和数据集泛化，包括无监督下涌现左侧通行行为。在InterPlan长尾基准上成为首个完全学习策略的领先者，超越更大的学习规划器；在常规驾驶val14基准上位列最佳方法之中且最安全，碰撞和时间碰撞分数最优。在Waymo开放模拟智能体真实感评估中，该方法优于其他无示范方法，与最强的参考锚定自博弈方法相当。同一训练栈可同时用于驾驶策略（支持汽车和卡车动力学）和模拟智能体（联合控制车辆、行人和骑行者）。

Conclusion: TerraZero成功构建了一个统一框架，能够同时支持高效的大规模强化学习训练和真实世界场景建模，在自动驾驶策略学习和模拟智能体生成两个任务上都取得了竞争性甚至领先性的性能，证明了纯强化学习自博弈训练方法在自动驾驶领域的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [143] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在处理长因果链的多球碰撞预测时性能下降，存在"序列性差距"问题：去噪过程无法提供可扩展的序列计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视频扩散模型在多球碰撞等需要长因果链推理的任务中的性能表现，探究性能下降的根本原因是否源于视频长度还是依赖事件结构。

Method: 设计对照实验：多球硬球动力学实验（存在球间交互）与长度匹配的单球控制实验（无球间交互）；进行干预研究，测试自回归/分块生成和架构深度等增加有效序列计算的方法。

Result: 标准双向视频扩散模型在因果链延长时性能下降，即使增加去噪步骤也无法改善；单球控制实验中性能下降基本消失，证明问题源于依赖事件结构而非视频长度；增加有效序列计算的方法能显著改善性能。

Conclusion: 识别出"序列性差距"模式：任务需要增长的序列计算与视频扩散模型去噪循环无法提供可扩展序列计算之间的不匹配；证明了对于确定性视频预测，去噪步骤不会增加骨干网络之外的序列计算，揭示了视频扩散在序列推理和仿真任务中的结构性障碍。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [HARP-ME: Closure-Driven Exact Induced Motif Enumeration on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12074)
*Ashwina Kumar,Rupesh Nasre*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了HARP-ME，一个用于精确枚举连通诱导四节点模体的GPU框架，通过闭包感知编译和诱导签名重用技术，在多种图上实现了比现有方法最高10.73倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确诱导模体枚举是图挖掘中的基础操作，但在GPU上仍面临挑战，包括候选扩展的不规则性、重复的集合交集主导执行，以及诱导计数必须同时考虑边的存在与缺失。当图数据超过GPU内存时，问题更加复杂。

Method: 提出HARP-ME框架，引入两个核心技术：(1)闭包感知编译，通过考虑遍历成本、代数推导收益、预期状态重用以及图分区带来的额外halo开销来选择锚点集进行显式枚举；(2)诱导签名重用，利用候选前沿信息和同时表示邻接与非邻接关系的紧凑约束来识别可重用的完成状态。对于超GPU内存的图，采用规范锚点所有者规则确保跨重叠halo区域分区的精确计数。

Result: 在六个社交、网络、生物和合成图上的实验表明，HARP-ME在所有评估方法中最快，相比Pangolin加速2.11倍，相比分区PBE加速1.83倍，相比CPU基线加速10.73倍。缓存命中率达到64%-76%，主机到设备的数据传输开销显著低于PBE风格分区。

Conclusion: 优化锚点选择以利用代数推导收益可以有效补充基于遍历和基于重用的GPU模体枚举技术，证明了将图元闭包恒等式集成到GPU执行框架中能够减少显式候选扩展和重复诱导边检查。

Abstract: Exact induced motif enumeration is a fundamental operation in graph mining, but it remains challenging on GPUs because candidate expansion is irregular, repeated set intersections dominate execution, and induced counting must consider both the presence and absence of edges. We present HARP-ME, which stands for Hierarchical Anchor-Reuse Partitioned Motif Enumeration. It is a GPU framework for the exact enumeration of connected induced four-node motifs. HARP-ME introduces closure-aware compilation, which selects a set of anchors for explicit enumeration by considering traversal cost, algebraic derivation benefits, expected state reuse, and the additional halo overhead caused by graph partitioning. HARP-ME also introduces induced-signature reuse. This technique identifies reusable completion states using candidate-frontier information together with compact constraints representing both adjacency and non-adjacency relationships. For graphs that exceed GPU memory, a canonical anchor-owner rule ensures exact counting across partitions with overlapping halo regions. We do not claim that the individual graphlet closure identities are new. Instead, our contribution is their integration into a GPU execution framework that reduces both explicit candidate expansion and repeated induced-edge checks. Across six social, web, biological, and synthetic graphs, HARP-ME is the fastest among the evaluated methods. It achieves up to 2.11 times speedup over Pangolin, up to 1.83 times speedup over partitioned PBE, and up to 10.73 times speedup over the evaluated CPU baseline. Detailed measurements show cache-hit rates between 64% and 76%, along with substantially lower host-to-device data transfer overhead than PBE-style partitioning. These results demonstrate that optimizing anchor selection for algebraic derivation benefits can complement traversal-based and reuse-based GPU motif enumeration techniques.

</details>


### [145] [Toward Trustworthy Autonomous Science: A Two-Year Community Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12113)
*Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Milad Abolhasani,Peter Beaucage,Laura Biven,Michael Bussmann,Kyle Chard,Ryan Coffee,Stephen DeWitt,Sagar Dolas,Carrie Eckert,David Elbert,Ian Foster,Tirthankar Ghosal,Anna Giannakou,Tom Gibbs,Leslie Hamilton,Glenn Lockwood,Theresa Mayer,Ben Mintz,Raffi Nazikian,Sal Nimer,Amanda Randles,Woong Shin,Sreenivas Rangan Sukumar,Frédéric Suter,Mitra Taheri,Michela Taufer,Draguna Vrabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是对一年前发布的AISLE路线图的更新，反映自主实验室领域的快速发展。发现领域面临关键张力：生成发现已不再困难，但验证发现成为新的瓶颈。将路线图从5个维度扩展到7个维度，将信任/验证/可复现性和安全/治理提升为核心地位，并制定了两年发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室领域在过去一年发展速度超出预期：多智能体系统已产出实验验证的假设、自驱动实验室互操作性增强、推理训练和领域基础模型提升能力上限、Genesis Mission将自主实验置于美国联邦科学战略核心。但同时出现反面趋势：旗舰发现结果被修正、基准测试显示智能体在开放式研究中表现有限、顶级会议出现伪造引用。这种"生成发现容易但验证困难"的不对称性成为限制自主科学发展的关键因素。

Method: 1. 回顾原路线图的五个关键维度，将其扩展为七个维度，将原有的跨领域关注点（信任/验证/可复现性、安全/治理）提升为独立维度；2. 评估原有里程碑M1-M14的实现状态（已实现、部分实现、重新定义或开放）；3. 新增四个里程碑M15-M18；4. 规划两年发展路径：第一年聚焦接口、协议采用和验证框架搭建，第二年针对联盟、零信任协调和治理；5. 定位草根网络作为互操作性架构，连接国家项目、国际倡议和商业平台。

Result: 1. 原有里程碑评估：M1-M14中部分已实现、部分实现、重新定义或保持开放状态；2. 新增四个里程碑M15-M18；3. 确定了验证与能力之间的不对称性为当前主要瓶颈；4. 提出两年实施计划：第一年聚焦接口、协议和验证脚手架，第二年聚焦联盟、零信任协调和治理；5. 明确草根网络作为连接各方的互操作性架构角色。

Conclusion: 自主实验室领域已进入新阶段，核心挑战从"生成发现"转向"验证发现"。必须将信任、验证、可复现性以及安全、治理从跨领域关注点提升为独立维度。未来两年需通过标准化接口和协议、建立验证框架、发展零信任协调机制和治理体系，将分散的自主实验室整合为互联互通的网络，而非重新形成信息孤岛。草根网络将成为连接国家项目、国际倡议和商业平台的互操作性基础设施。

Abstract: One year ago, the AISLE roadmap argued that autonomous laboratories operated as isolated islands and proposed a grassroots network organized around five critical dimensions. The field has since moved faster than anticipated. Multi-agent systems have produced experimentally validated hypotheses, self-driving laboratories have grown more interoperable and orchestrated, reasoning-trained and domain foundation models have raised the capability ceiling, and the Genesis Mission has placed autonomous experimentation at the center of U.S. federal science strategy, with industry emerging as a primary actor. Progress has met a sobering counter-current, including a corrected flagship discovery result, benchmarks showing that agents which rival experts on closed-ended questions still complete only a fraction of open-ended research, and fabricated citations surfacing at leading venues. We read this as the defining tension of the field. Producing a candidate discovery is no longer the hard part, but verifying it is, and this asymmetry now limits autonomous science more than raw model capability. We update the roadmap around seven dimensions, revisiting the original five and elevating two former cross-cutting concerns, trust, verification, and reproducibility, and safety, security, and governance, to first-class status. We assess the original milestones (M1 through M14) as achieved, partially achieved, reframed, or open, add four new milestones (M15 through M18), and scope the path forward to a two-year horizon. The first year concentrates on interfaces, protocol adoption, and the scaffolding of verification, and the second targets federation, zero-trust coordination, and governance. Throughout, we position the grassroots network as the interoperability fabric that lets national programs, international initiatives, and commercial platforms connect rather than re-silo.

</details>


### [146] [FlashDiff: Efficient Regional Execution and Scheduling for Diffusion Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12121)
*Yaqi Qiao,Ping He,Songrun Xie,Ayush Barik,Chensong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashDiff通过自适应区域执行和调度优化扩散模型推理，利用扩散过程中不同区域稳定速度不同和步骤间的时间相关性，选择性执行需要进一步优化的区域，显著降低延迟并提高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像、视频和音频生成中被广泛采用，但其推理服务效率面临挑战。扩散推理需要在多个去噪步骤中重复更新高维空间或时间隐变量，导致生成延迟高且限制了服务吞吐量。现有的多GPU并行化方法虽然能减少单步计算，但会引入显著的激活交换开销，导致通信成本抵消甚至超过并行执行的收益。

Method: FlashDiff提出了三个核心机制：1) 利用早期阶段的注意力信号将隐变量表示分解为连贯的执行区域，在保留语义结构的同时暴露细粒度并行性；2) 使用轻量级运行时控制器估计区域活动，当进一步优化不太可能影响输出质量时跳过低影响更新；3) 应用亲和性感知的在线调度器，将依赖区域共置，平衡GPU间的剩余负载，并重用回收的计算能力以提高服务效率。该方法基于扩散优化在隐变量区域和去噪步骤间不均匀的观察。

Result: 在真实世界的图像、视频和音频工作负载上，FlashDiff将端到端服务延迟降低30-97%，并将吞吐量提高1.2-2.2倍。

Conclusion: FlashDiff成功通过自适应区域执行和调度策略解决了扩散模型推理服务的效率瓶颈，证明了利用扩散过程中的非均匀特性和时间相关性可以显著提升服务性能，为扩散模型的实际部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the central backbone for modern image, video, and audio generation, but their efficient service remains a challenge. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion inference repeatedly updates high-dimensional spatial or temporal latents over many denoising steps. This all-region execution pattern makes generation latency high and limits serving throughput. Existing multi-GPU parallelization methods can reduce per-step computation, but often introduce substantial activation exchange overhead, causing communication to offset or even outweigh the benefits of parallel execution.
  This paper presents FlashDiff, a diffusion serving system that improves inference efficiency through adaptive regional execution and scheduling. FlashDiff is based on the observation that diffusion refinement is not uniform across latent regions or denoising steps: different regions often stabilize at different rates, while neighboring steps exhibit strong temporal correlation. FlashDiff leverages these properties to selectively execute only regions that require further refinement and to reallocate the resulting compute slack across concurrent serving requests. FlashDiff consists of three mechanisms. First, it decomposes the latent representation into coherent execution regions using early-stage attention signals, preserving semantic structure while exposing fine-grained parallelism. Second, it uses a lightweight runtime controller to estimate region activity and bypass low-impact updates when further refinement is unlikely to affect output quality. Third, it applies an affinity-aware online scheduler that co-locates dependent regions, balances residual load across GPUs, and reuses reclaimed compute capacity to improve serving efficiency. Across real-world image, video, and audio workloads, FlashDiff reduces end-to-end serving latency by 30-97% and improves throughput by 1.2-2.2x.

</details>


### [147] [Decentralized Gradient Descent: Bottleneck Regimes and Budget Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12172)
*Nicolò Michelusi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从资源感知角度研究分布式梯度下降算法，识别出初始化、目标异质性、网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声作为不同误差尺度下的主导因素，提出DNR和GCR两个关键指标来刻画优化过程中的瓶颈序列和最优资源预算策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对分散式梯度下降（DGD）算法的研究主要关注收敛性质，但缺乏对达到指定精度所需的通信和计算资源的系统性理解。在分布式网络中，资源受限是一个关键约束，因此需要从资源感知的角度研究DGD算法，明确达到目标误差水平所需的通信-计算预算。

Method: 提出了一种以瓶颈为中心的分析框架，该框架认识到不同误差尺度下不同因素主导优化动态。引入了两个基本量：梯度多样性-网络连接性比（DNR）和梯度-通信噪声比（GCR）。采用多阶段分析方法，识别出初始化、目标异质性、网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声等主导因素，推导出最优步长选择和明确的预算复杂度界限。

Result: 获得了达到指定精度所需的预算资源的明确界限表达式，揭示了总预算如何分解为与连续瓶颈相关的贡献。结果表明DNR和GCR这两个量决定了优化过程中遇到的瓶颈序列以及相应的预算最优操作策略。研究量化了目标异质性、网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声之间的基本权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为分布式优化中的资源分配提供了理论基础，通过识别不同误差尺度下的主导瓶颈因素，给出了预算最优的策略指导。所提出的分析框架和关键指标为理解和优化分布式梯度下降算法的资源效率提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Decentralized gradient descent (DGD) is widely used for solving distributed optimization problems over networks of agents. While its convergence properties are well understood, less is known about the communication and computation resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. In this paper, we study DGD from a resource-aware perspective and characterize the communication-computation budget required to attain a target error level. We develop a bottleneck-centric framework in which different factors dominate the optimization dynamics at different error scales. Specifically, we identify operating regimes governed by initialization, objective heterogeneity and network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise. To capture these effects, we introduce two fundamental quantities: the gradient-Diversity-to-Network-connectivity Ratio (DNR) and the Gradient-to-Communication-noise Ratio (GCR). We show that these quantities determine the sequence of bottlenecks encountered during optimization and the corresponding budget-optimal operating strategy. Using a multi-stage analysis, we derive optimal stepsize selections and explicit budget-complexity bounds that quantify the budget resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. The resulting expressions reveal how the overall budget decomposes into contributions associated with successive bottlenecks and provide insight into the fundamental tradeoffs among objective heterogeneity, network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise.

</details>


### [148] [Overcoming Orchestration Bottlenecks at Exascale: A Decentralized, Policy-Driven Approach for Sim-AI Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12211)
*Harikrishna Tummalapalli,Christine M. Simpson,Riccardo Balin,Vitali A. Morozov,Thang D. Pham,Murat Keceli,Thomas D. Uram*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了EnsembleLauncher，一种面向百亿亿次计算系统的递归分层工作流编排器，通过去中心化控制平面和可编程调度策略接口，在Aurora超级计算机上成功扩展至800万个串行任务，性能超越现有最优工具4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正从单体应用转向耦合仿真-AI工作流，这类工作流由高度异构的任务组成，具有不同的硬件、规模和运行时需求。随着工作流扩展到领导级系统，极端的集成规模和任务变异性会导致编排瓶颈。现有系统级调度器吞吐量受限，而工作流工具由于僵化的控制平面拓扑和静态调度启发式方法面临可扩展性问题。

Method: 设计并实现了EnsembleLauncher，一种递归分层的工作流编排器，其核心特性包括：(1)完全去中心化的控制平面架构；(2)可编程的调度策略接口，支持灵活的调度策略定制。该系统专为百亿亿次计算系统设计，能够处理大规模异构任务编排。

Result: 在Aurora超级计算机上进行了实验验证，EnsembleLauncher成功扩展到整个机器规模，支持高达800万个串行任务。性能方面，相比现有最优工具实现了超过4倍的性能提升。此外，通过可编程调度接口展示了调度策略对高方差集成和主动学习管道资源利用率的显著影响。

Conclusion: EnsembleLauncher有效解决了大规模异构工作流编排的可扩展性瓶颈问题，证明了去中心化控制平面和可编程调度策略在百亿亿次计算系统中的重要价值。该工作为现代耦合仿真-AI工作流的高效执行提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Scientific computing is increasingly shifting from monolithic applications to coupled simulation-AI workflows composed of highly heterogeneous tasks with diverse hardware, scale, and runtime requirements. As these workflows scale to leadership-class systems, the resulting extreme ensemble sizes and task variability can create orchestration bottlenecks. System-level schedulers are often configured for limited throughput, while workflow tools face scalability issues due to rigid control-plane topologies and static scheduling heuristics. We introduce EnsembleLauncher, a recursively hierarchical workflow orchestrator for exascale systems, featuring a fully decentralized control plane and a programmable scheduling policy interface. On the Aurora supercomputer, EnsembleLauncher successfully scales to the entire machine with up to eight million serial tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art tools by more than four times. Additionally, we implement a programmable scheduling interface and demonstrate a significant impact of scheduling policies on resource utilization for high-variance ensembles and active learning pipelines representative of modern coupled simulation-AI workflows.

</details>


### [149] [Profiling and Scheduling Complex O-RAN Applications Across the 5G Edge and Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12230)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了O-DAG框架，用于解决O-RAN中AI/ML流水线在分布式基础设施上的执行成本分析、调度和验证问题，通过实验发现HEFT算法在所有配置下获得最低完成时间，但调度器性能依赖于工作负载特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN范式将智能RAN控制分解为相互依赖的AI/ML函数流水线（包括流量预测、信号质量估计和切片调度），这些函数需要在异构延迟和带宽约束下跨远边缘、近边缘和云资源的分散连续体执行。尽管这些流水线自然地表达为有向无环图(DAG)，但缺乏集成的方法来分析其执行成本、通过调度启发式将它们映射到分布式基础设施，并在5G蜂窝网络条件下验证结果放置。

Method: O-DAG框架包含四个紧密耦合阶段：(1) DagProfiler工具，用于检测O-RAN切片调度器并提取每个任务的指令计数和每条边的通信量；(2) 参数化的三层网络拓扑，编码远边缘(DU, RIC)、近边缘和云节点及其真实链路带宽；(3) SAGA调度框架的扩展；(4) 基于MintEDGE模拟器的自定义DAG仿真模块。评估了五种调度算法(HEFT, MCT, MinMin, MaxMin, Duplex)。

Result: 在切片调度应用上评估，配置跨度为5K-50K个UE、2-20个小区和2-10个网络切片。HEFT在所有配置下实现最低完成时间，但调度器排名依赖于工作负载。SAGA-仿真差距作为诊断指标：负差距(高达-1.72%)识别计算主导配置，其中HEFT保守估计；正差距(+0.64%)在低切片数时暴露通信受限场景，带宽争用超过调度模型假设。

Conclusion: 成功展示了端到端框架解决O-RAN DAG流水线调度问题的可行性，提供了调度算法性能的工作负载依赖性见解，建立了SAGA-仿真差距作为计算主导与通信主导场景的诊断工具，并开源所有构件以确保可复现性。

Abstract: The O-RAN paradigm decomposes intelligent RAN control into pipelines of interdependent AI/ML functions, including traffic prediction, signal quality estimation, and slice scheduling, that must execute across a dispersed continuum of far-edge, near-edge, and cloud resources under heterogeneous latency and bandwidth constraints. Despite the natural expression of these pipelines as Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs), no integrated methodology exists to profile their execution costs, map them onto dispersed infrastructure via scheduling heuristics, and validate the resulting placement under 5G cellular conditions. We present O-DAG, an end-to-end framework that closes this gap through four tightly coupled stages: (1) DagProfiler, a new open-source tool that instruments O-RAN Slice Scheduler and extracts per-task instruction counts and per-edge communication volumes; (2) a parameterized three-tier network topology encoding far-edge (DU, RIC), near-edge (edge), and cloud nodes with realistic link bandwidths; (3) an extension of the SAGA scheduling framework and (4) a custom DAG simulation module built on the MintEDGE simulator. We evaluate five scheduling algorithms (HEFT, MCT, MinMin, MaxMin, Duplex) for a slice scheduling application across various configurations spanning 5K--50K UEs, 2--20 cells, and 2--10 network slices. HEFT achieves the lowest makespan in all configurations, but scheduler rankings are workload-dependent. The SAGA--simulation gap serves as a regime diagnostic: negative gaps (up to -1.72%) identify compute-dominated configurations where HEFT overestimates conservatively, while a positive gap (+0.64%) at low slice counts exposes a communication-bound regime where bandwidth contention exceeds the scheduling model's assumptions. All artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [150] [Parallelizing Legacy Mesh Generation Software: Lessons Learned from a Pseudo-Constrained Parallel Data Refinement Approach for Advancing Front Local Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12253)
*Kevin Garner,David Marcum,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过将遗留软件AFLR作为黑盒进行并行化，实现了11倍加速，但发现黑盒并行化方法受限于输入边界约束，导致稳定性和可扩展性不足，建议采用无约束的并行化设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对遗留科学计算软件AFLR（Advancing Front Local Reconnection）的并行化需求，探索黑盒方法将现有串行网格生成代码并行化的可行性和局限性。

Method: 采用数据分解策略将计算域划分为多个子域，每个子域使用串行网格生成代码并行细化，配合运行时系统实现负载均衡；整体将AFLR视为黑盒进行并行化处理。

Result: 在16个CPU核心上实现了约11倍加速，输出网格质量良好；但受限于黑盒的输入边界要求，无法达到完全稳定性（与串行方法相同的网格质量）和理想可扩展性；每个子域的边界约束增加了开销并导致输出网格体积与串行方法不同。

Conclusion: 黑盒遗留代码的并行化可能不切实际，建议采用无此类约束的并行化设计方法，以高效利用大规模架构提供的并行性。

Abstract: This paper presents lessons learned from parallelizing the legacy software known as Advancing Front Local Reconnection (AFLR) as a black box. The parallel procedure utilizes (i) a data decomposition scheme where each subdomain is refined in parallel using the sequential mesh generation code and (ii) a runtime system for work-load balancing. Results on the mesh refinement operation show that the parallel method's stability (output mesh quality) is good and that the parallel method outperforms the serial AFLR by about 11 times when utilizing 16 CPU cores. However, full stability (i.e., generating the same quality as the serial method) and potential scalability is limited due to the constraints set by the black box's input boundary requirement. Satisfying this requirement for each subdomain not only adds overhead but also causes the parallel method to generate a different output mesh volume than that generated by the serial method. The complexity of such a state-of-the-art code requires that it be modified to a non-trivial extent in order to remove these constraints. These results suggest that the parallelization of black-box legacy codes like AFLR may not be practical and instead encourages an approach that is originally designed without such constraints to efficiently leverage the concurrency offered by large-scale architectures.

</details>


### [151] [Scaling Synthetic-Image Pre-Training for Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12583)
*Qianpiao Ma,Xiaozhu Song,Junlong Zhou,Yue Zeng,Jianchun Liu,Huaqing Tu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FeDiSyn统一框架，通过联合优化预训练与联邦微调，解决大视觉模型联邦微调中的资源限制、系统异构性和非独立同分布数据挑战，实现训练时间减少52.5%、通信成本降低97.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦微调方法存在三个关键局限：(1) 现有合成图像生成方法无法捕获设备特定的特征分布；(2) 当前基于PEFT的联邦微调方法低估了可能提供关键信息的弱设备贡献；(3) 预训练与联邦微调的分离优化忽视了二者的内在联系，缺乏整体视角以最大化训练效率。

Method: 提出FeDiSyn统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 建立联邦微调预训练的缩放定律，确定最优合成图像数量，平衡预训练收益与生成/预训练成本；(2) 基于扩散模型的合成图像生成方法，捕获设备特定特征分布用于预训练，以解决非独立同分布数据问题；(3) 贡献感知的LoRA配置与带宽分配算法，确保有信息量的设备被有效利用，同时解决系统异构性问题。

Result: 在真实世界测试平台上验证，FeDiSyn相比现有解决方案：(1) 完成时间减少超过52.5%；(2) 通信成本降低超过97.2%；(3) 达到与最先进方法相当的准确率。

Conclusion: FeDiSyn成功实现了预训练与联邦微调的整体联合优化，通过缩放定律指导、设备特定合成图像生成以及贡献感知的资源分配，有效解决了大视觉模型联邦微调中的资源约束、系统异构性和非独立同分布数据三大核心挑战，为隐私敏感场景下的大规模视觉模型部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning (FedFT) enables adapting pre-trained large vision models (LVMs) on distributed, privacy-sensitive devices, while its practical deployment is hindered by three critical challenges: resource constraints, system heterogeneity, and non-IID data. While prior studies partially address these issues, e.g., by pre-training initial models on synthetic images to mitigate the adverse effects of non-IID data, or leveraging parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to reduce resource consumption, they remain inadequate and fragmented. Specifically, existing synthetic image generation methods fail to capture device-specific feature distributions, while current PEFT-based FedFT methods often undervalue weaker devices that may provide critical information. More importantly, the separate optimization of pre-training and FedFT neglects their inherent connection, lacking a holistic perspective to maximize training efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose FeDiSyn, a unified framework that holistically considers the interplay between pre-training and FedFT to minimize the overall LVM training time. Specifically, FeDiSyn introduces: (i) a scaling law for FedFT pre-training to determine the optimal number of synthetic images, balancing pre-training benefit against generation/pre-training cost, (ii) diffusion-based synthetic image generation that captures device-specific feature distributions for pre-training to tackle non-IID data, and (iii) a contribution-aware LoRA configuration and bandwidth allocation algorithm for FedFT to ensure that informative devices are effectively utilized while addressing system heterogeneity. Experimental results on the real-world testbed demonstrate that FeDiSyn reduces completion time by over 52.5% and communication cost by over 97.2%, while achieving comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [152] [HeteroMosaic: Exposing and Exploiting Heterogeneous Execution Opportunities for Energy-Efficient Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12839)
*Gregory Hyegang Jun,Wesley Pang,Eddie Richter,Mehdi Saeedi,Aporva Amarnath,Pallavi Ferrao,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HeteroMosaic提出了一种面向边缘LLM推理的异构优先调度框架，通过异构Roofline模型识别iGPU与NPU协同执行的收益，并采用依赖保持的微批次分解与跟踪引导的协同优化策略，在三种AMD Ryzen AI平台上实现了最高2.35倍性能提升与45.3%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM运行时通常采用粗粒度的设备级决策或孤立地优化算子，导致异构资源利用不足。特别是在统一内存平台上，性能同时依赖于设备放置与任务图协调，而现有方法忽视了iGPU与NPU之间的协同潜力及实际执行中的内存竞争、DVFS、设备差异等因素。

Method: HeteroMosaic首先利用异构Roofline模型识别iGPU与NPU结合执行是否有益；然后将推理分解为保持依赖关系的微批次，暴露跨加速器重叠机会；最后采用跟踪引导的方法协同优化调度与设备分配，同时考虑内存竞争、DVFS、设备变化及NPU运行时开销等实际效应。

Result: 在平衡型AMD Ryzen AI平台上，HeteroMosaic相比iGPU基线实现最高1.73倍加速，相比NPU基线实现1.78倍加速，相比llama.cpp等框架实现2.05倍加速，同时能耗降低最高45.3%。相比先前异构边缘AI解决方案，性能提升最高达2.35倍。

Conclusion: HeteroMosaic证明了异构优先调度策略能有效提升边缘LLM推理的性能与能效，通过细粒度的微批次分解与跨设备协同优化，充分挖掘了统一内存平台中iGPU与NPU的协同潜力，为异构边缘AI系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern edge system-on-chips (SoCs) combine CPUs, integrated GPUs (iGPUs), and neural processing units (NPUs), yet existing LLM runtimes typically make coarse device-level decisions or optimize operators in isolation. As a result, they underutilize heterogeneous resources, particularly on unified-memory platforms where performance depends on both device placement and task-graph coordination.
  We present HeteroMosaic, a heterogeneity-first scheduling framework for edge LLM inference. HeteroMosaic first uses a heterogeneous roofline model to identify when combining iGPU and NPU execution is beneficial. It then decomposes inference into dependency-preserving micro-batches that expose cross-accelerator overlap and applies trace-guided co-optimization of scheduling and device allocation under practical effects such as memory contention, DVFS, device variation, and NPU runtime overheads.
  We implement HeteroMosaic in PyTorch C++ and evaluate it on three AMD Ryzen AI platforms spanning NPU-heavy, balanced, and iGPU-heavy designs. On the balanced platform, HeteroMosaic achieves up to 1.73X speedup over an iGPU baseline, 1.78X over an NPU baseline, and 2.05X over frameworks such as \texttt{llama.cpp}, while reducing energy by up to 45.3%. It also improves performance over prior heterogeneous edge AI solutions by up to 2.35X.

</details>


### [153] [Proceedings of HLPP 2026: 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12917)
*Chong Li,Corinne Ancourt,Gaétan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This volume contains the ten peer-reviewed papers presented at HLPP 2026, the 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications, held on 9-10 July 2026 at the Institut Henri Poincare in Paris, France. The symposium covers high-level approaches to parallel programming: programming models, languages, libraries, algorithmic skeletons, compilers, and runtime systems for multi-core, GPU, and distributed platforms. The 2026 edition extended this scope towards artificial intelligence, with new topics on the parallel programming and performance of AI systems and on AI-assisted generation of parallel code, and opened with a dedicated session on parallelism and AI. Papers were selected through a single-blind review process, with three Program Committee reviews per submission, and appear in the order of the symposium program. Edited by Chong Li, Corinne Ancourt, and Gaetan Hains.

</details>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [SkillComm: Skill-Driven Semantic Communication for Sequential Workflows via Incremental Token Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11972)
*Ziyang Meng,Lu Lu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SkillComm框架，通过复用技能状态实现工作流感知的令牌选择，在保持99.4%精度的同时减少51.2%的传输开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度联合信源信道编码（DeepJSCC）和提示引导视觉传输方案将每个任务视为独立的完整令牌传输，缺乏跨语义工作流的执行记忆复用。对于检测-分割-关键点等工作流，后续阶段往往仅需状态相关的语义更新，全量传输效率低下。

Method: 提出SkillComm技能驱动语义通信框架：1）共享Skill-Book将高层视觉意图映射为收发端同步的可执行技能序列；2）基于工作流条件化，自适应令牌选择利用跨步骤记忆，仅通过联合信源信道编码传输状态活跃令牌；3）接收端将解码令牌与本地历史记忆融合，重建任务就绪的令牌网格。

Result: 在MS COCO 2017验证集的检测-分割-关键点工作流实验中，SkillComm在高信噪比条件下将令牌传输开销降低51.2%，同时保持99.4%的上界归一化平均精度。

Conclusion: 可复用技能状态能够实现选择性语义更新交付，为未来智能体与具身视觉智能提供高效的语义通信方案。

Abstract: As wireless visual intelligence evolves from isolated task inference to ordered skill workflows, the communication bottleneck shifts from transmitting a single semantic representation to coordinating reusable skill states under channel constraints. Existing DeepJSCC and prompt-guided visual transmitters usually treat each task as an independent full-token transmission, with limited reuse of execution memory across semantic workflows. This is inefficient for workflows such as Detect, Segment, and Keypoint, where later stages often require only state-relevant semantic updates. To this end, we propose SkillComm, a skill-driven semantic communication framework that uses reusable skill states as shared context for workflow-aware token prioritization and memory-assisted token-grid reconstruction. A shared Skill-Book maps a high-level visual intent into a synchronized executable skill sequence at the transmitter and receiver. Conditioned on this workflow, adaptive token selection exploits cross-step memory to transmit only state-active tokens through joint source-channel coding, while the receiver reconstructs a task-ready token grid by combining decoded tokens with local historical memory. Experiments on the MS COCO 2017 validation set for the Detect-Segment-Keypoint workflow show that SkillComm reduces token transmission cost by 51.2% while retaining 99.4% upper-bound-normalized average precision at high SNR. These results demonstrate that reusable skill states enable selective semantic update delivery for future agentic and embodied visual intelligence.

</details>


### [155] [OSNR/GSNR Prediction in Brownfield Links via a DLM-Anchored Hybrid Physics/ML Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12152)
*Agastya Raj,Venkata Virajit Garbhapu,Hiroyuki Ishihara,Peyman Pahlevanzadeh,Hideki Nishizawa,Takeo Sasai,Daniel C. Kilper,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DLM锚定的混合物理/机器学习框架，用于棕地光纤链路的性能预测，实现了对单通道和OSaaS场景下OSNR/GSNR误差分别不超过0.39/0.43 dB的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对棕地光纤网络中准确预测各信道功率、光信噪比(OSNR)和广义信噪比(GSNR)的需求，传统物理模型在复杂网络环境中精度有限，纯机器学习方法缺乏可解释性，因此需要开发一种结合物理模型和机器学习优势的混合框架。

Method: 提出DLM(数字线性模型)锚定的混合物理/机器学习框架，通过DLM校准跨段/在线放大器(ILA)边界，结合物理模型的可解释性和机器学习的非线性拟合能力，实现对每通道功率、OSNR和GSNR的准确预测。

Result: 在单通道和OSaaS(按需光服务)配置场景下，该框架实现的OSNR预测误差不超过0.39 dB，GSNR预测误差不超过0.43 dB，验证了方法的高精度性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的DLM锚定混合框架成功解决了棕地光网络链路的性能预测问题，实现了高精度的信道质量指标预测，为光网络智能化运维和优化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We present a DLM-anchored hybrid physics/ML framework for brownfield optical links that accurately predicts per-channel power, OSNR, and GSNR. Calibrating span/ILA boundaries via DLM yields OSNR/GSNR errors of no more than 0.39/0.43 dB across single-channel and OSaaS provisioning.

</details>


### [156] [High-Precision Hybrid FA-PSO Based Inversion of Building Material Parameters for Fundamental Wireless Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12721)
*Zhuowei Li,Yalei Zhu,Hanqing Zhang,Sui Li,Meng Chen,Tong Zhang,Zi-Yang Wu,Dan Yang,Songjiang Yang,Jiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应萤火虫粒子群优化算法的反演方法，用于精确估计建筑材料的电磁参数（介电常数、电导率和厚度），其估计精度接近理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取建筑材料的电磁参数（介电常数、电导率和厚度）对于评估其无线通信性能至关重要。现有的自由空间测量方法需要高效且稳健的反演算法来提高参数估计的精度和收敛效率。

Method: 提出基于萤火虫粒子群优化(FA-PSO)算法的反演方法。采用自适应萤火虫算法(FA)系统优化粒子群优化(PSO)的超参数，以提高收敛效率和稳健性。通过优化用于种群初始化的高斯分布参数，逐步提高参数估计精度。推导了复高斯噪声模型下的克拉美-罗下界(CRLB)作为理论基准。

Result: 数值结果表明，对于相对较薄的材料，所提方法的估计精度接近理论下界(CRLB)，验证了反演框架的有效性。通过优化算法参数，成功实现了对建筑材料介电常数、电导率和厚度的精确估计。

Conclusion: 本研究准确提取了建筑材料的电磁特性，为评估其无线性能提供了有力支持。所提出的FA-PSO反演框架具有良好的估计精度和收敛性能，为建筑材料电磁参数测量提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an inversion method based on the firefly particle swarm optimization (FA-PSO) algorithm to estimate the permittivity, conductivity, and thickness of building materials using the free-space method. To improve convergence efficiency and robustness, an adaptive firefly algorithm (FA) is employed to systematically optimize the hyperparameters of the particle swarm optimization (PSO). By optimizing the parameters of the Gaussian distribution used for population initialization, the accuracy of parameter estimation is gradually improved. Furthermore, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for the permittivity, conductivity, and thickness under a complex Gaussian noise model, which serves as a theoretical benchmark for evaluating the estimation accuracy of the FA-PSO algorithm. Numerical results indicate that for relatively thin materials, the estimation accuracy of the proposed method approaches this theoretical lower bound, confirming the effectiveness of the inversion framework. This study accurately extracts the electromagnetic properties of building materials, providing strong support for evaluating their wireless performance.

</details>
